- **spec-reviewer**: Verify a code change matches its stated requirements. Use when reviewing an implementation against a spec, task brief, issue, or user request. ([trevorwhitney/tw-agent-plugin](https://github.com/trevorwhitney/tw-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Per-task spec compliance reviewer. Verifies implementation matches the task specification exactly — nothing missing, nothing extra. Used during subagent-driven-development. MUST BE USED after each task. ([Vatsal2401/Auto-Reels-Backend](https://github.com/Vatsal2401/Auto-Reels-Backend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a design spec for one named aspect, or reads it in full for blocking defects, and writes a findings file. ([ViktorGozhiy/ship-happens](https://github.com/ViktorGozhiy/ship-happens/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Review SDD feature specifications for Kotlin/Spring/ArrowKt projects for completeness, consistency, testability, and Clean Architecture compliance. ([wakita181009/clean-architecture-kotlin](https://github.com/wakita181009/clean-architecture-kotlin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Validate implementation against task acceptance criteria. Checks each criterion individually and reports which are met. Used by astra orchestrator evaluator loop. ([weardo/astra](https://github.com/weardo/astra/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: PR作成前に、実装セッションとは別のコンテキストで「origin/developとの差分」と「担当issue・spec.mdの受け入れ条件」を突き合わせる読み取り専用のレビュアー。受け入れ条件の充足・テストの有無・担当範囲外の変更・正確性の欠陥・層の責務違反だけを重大度付きで報告する。 ([wknd-studio/brand-closed-ec](https://github.com/wknd-studio/brand-closed-ec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: The second review axis: checks the PR diff against the original task (task file / issue / brief). Three questions — what was asked and is missing, what is present and was not asked (scope creep), what looks done but is done wrong. Does not look at code correctness (that is code-reviewer) and does not look at security (that is security-reviewer). Dispatched on EVERY PR that has an original task. Output in English. ([yaremenko-maksym/CheekyCheeseIT_CRM](https://github.com/yaremenko-maksym/CheekyCheeseIT_CRM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Review the complete change (proposal -&gt; design -&gt; specs -&gt; tasks), assess verifiability, and output a structured review report. Also handles escalation mode — root-cause analysis when a task fails 3 fix attempts. ([Yuchenhui/harness-spec](https://github.com/Yuchenhui/harness-spec/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Review specification consistency. Detect over-engineering and unnecessary defensive code. ([YunosukeYoshino/harness](https://github.com/YunosukeYoshino/harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks an implementation against its spec and flags every acceptance criterion that is not fully met. Use after /implement to validate before merging. Input: the spec file path and the implementation file paths to audit. Output: a structured verdict table (PASS / FAIL / PARTIAL / UNVERIFIABLE) with a specific code citation for every criterion. ([yuvarajd85/ai-ml-learnings-exemplars](https://github.com/yuvarajd85/ai-ml-learnings-exemplars/blob/HEAD/claude-projects/ticketflow-Demo/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only critique of a drafted Spec before accept. Produces structured findings and questions only. ([yzlin/supa-pi](https://github.com/yzlin/supa-pi/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: First-pass spec reviewer (Uber "first-pass PRD" style). Scores a normalized spec across fixed dimensions, classifies its risk, and returns a launch-readiness verdict with a single "start here" fix, prioritized Critical/Optimization action items, and write-ready replacement text. Read-only; runs before the human spec gate. ([zd87pl/cc-loop-boilerplate](https://github.com/zd87pl/cc-loop-boilerplate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks behaviour, schemas, and delivery claims against the numbered ContextOS specification. Use before closing a requirement or claiming a phase gate. ([zentelligence/contextos](https://github.com/zentelligence/contextos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: 规格/计划文档审查。触发词：审查 spec、审查计划、doc review。 ([1075551341/claude_code_config](https://github.com/1075551341/claude_code_config/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Use after finishing a task, plan or branch to review a git range against its plan, spec or requirements before merging. Read-only; returns severity-ranked issues and a merge verdict. Not for a general diff review with no plan (use the engineering code-reviewer). ([123yogin/the-agency](https://github.com/123yogin/the-agency/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-workflow/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 在 OpenSpec change 的需求文件(proposal/design/tasks/specs)寫完後 呼叫,在進入實作前審查。檢查需求完整性、邊界情況、安全與權限需求、AC 與測試 任務的雙向對應、與既有規格的一致性,並對文件中對既有程式碼行為的具體事實 宣稱做有限度的原始碼核對。不評論程式碼風格或實作細節,那是 strict-reviewer 的工作。 ([a620906209/CFDivePlatform](https://github.com/a620906209/CFDivePlatform/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec compliance reviewer. Checks that implementation matches Phase 3 design — nothing more, nothing less. READ-ONLY — cannot modify code. ([AAnkacHH/ankach-dev-framework](https://github.com/AAnkacHH/ankach-dev-framework/blob/HEAD/workflows/dev-pipeline/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks a change against memoirist's README promises, specs (API_CONTRACT, DATABASE_SCHEMA, DECISIONS, USER_FLOWS) and rules (security, api, backend, frontend). Use after finishing a sprint task or before committing, e.g. "have spec-reviewer check this task". ([aarrroon/memoirist](https://github.com/aarrroon/memoirist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Stage 1 of the SDD two-stage review gate. Runs after every implementer run and BEFORE code-reviewer. Verifies the implementation matches the spec exactly — nothing more, nothing less — by reading actual code, not trusting the implementer's report. Does NOT evaluate code quality. Read-only. Adapted from obra/superpowers SDD. ([Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023](https://github.com/Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 收片用的「Spec 轴」只读审查:输入任务书(或契约)+ diff,只报三类——要求了没做 / 没要求却做了 / 做了但看起来不对,每条引任务书原句。不审 bug 不审风格(那是 /code-review 的轴),不改任何文件。 ([AbyssCN/claude-lead-harness](https://github.com/AbyssCN/claude-lead-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a diff against the spec it was meant to implement (the linked issue, the plan the user approved, or a spec file the caller names) and reports missing or partial requirements, unrequested behaviour, and requirements implemented wrongly, each quoting its spec line. The spec axis of /agent-core:review. Changes no file. ([adhibuchori/agent-config-kit](https://github.com/adhibuchori/agent-config-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-core/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review an already-written feature spec under specs/ for ambiguities, uncovered edge cases, and contradictions with other specs or with docs/requirements.md. Complements, and does not replace, /speckit-clarify (ambiguity resolution on the current spec before planning) and /speckit-analyze (spec/plan/tasks consistency within one feature) — this agent's job is adversarial re-reading of a spec that already exists, and checking it against every other spec, not just itself. ([adrisons/gym-log-app](https://github.com/adrisons/gym-log-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a spec in specs/ before any code is written. Use when a new or changed spec is ready for implementation. ([AhmadSaleh22/footprint-to-ifc](https://github.com/AhmadSaleh22/footprint-to-ifc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec-compliance reviewer. It checks a diff against its task's written requirements (missing, extra or misread) without trusting the implementer's report. ([aidan-bailey/loom](https://github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/blob/HEAD/flow/plugin/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review a feature's spec.md, plan.md, tasks.md or fix.md in specs/, or a requirements.md in docs/requirements/, against the constitution and higher-level documents. - **spec-reviewer**: Checks that a Person Service PR delivers exactly its slice from the develop-phase table and docs/plan.md, against the ~400-line Java target. Use on every PR. - **spec-reviewer**: Judges whether one written specification may enter development — free of self-contradiction, current with the code, complete from itself alone, and testable — against the closed admission register. - **spec-reviewer**: Checks a diff or file set against its spec sources — linked issues, their workspaces, and domain spec docs. - **spec-reviewer**: Spec-fidelity reviewer. ([ViniciusNyp/dotfiles](https://github.com/ViniciusNyp/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only spec-compliance reviewer with fresh context. Use from the pre-pr skill. Gets an issue number and a diff range. Checks that each Acceptance item of the issue has code and a failing-without-it test in the diff, and that no test or value is faked. Reports findings with file and line evidence. Never edits files. ([vpr1995/prudhvi-software-factory](https://github.com/vpr1995/prudhvi-software-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: spec.md（仕様書）の品質をレビューし、改善点を報告する。 concept-summary.md と spec.md のパスを受け取り、Read で読み込んでレビュー結果を出力する。 &lt;example&gt; user: "仕様書をレビューして" assistant: [spec.md と concept-summary.md を読み込み、spec-reviewer エージェントを起動] &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; user: "spec.md の品質チェックをして" assistant: [spec.md を読み込み、spec-reviewer エージェントを起動] &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; user: "仕様の抜け漏れを確認して" assistant: [concept-summary.md と spec.md を読み込み、spec-reviewer エージェントを起動] &lt;/example&gt; ([wanx2land/agents-spec-driven](https://github.com/wanx2land/agents-spec-driven/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Checkpoint-review worker for spec-review dispatch mode. Spawned at a checkpoint's declared reviewer floor in a fresh, isolated context; reads the checkpoint contract, journal, and the full diff (or artifact), and returns spec-review's structured findings (blocker/important/advisory) plus a fixed-format verdict as its final message. The coordinating session writes the outcome back to spec and journal. Not for interactive or general use. ([waseric/ai-tools](https://github.com/waseric/ai-tools/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a task's diff against the task text: everything asked for, nothing extra ([wireframe/qrspi](https://github.com/wireframe/qrspi/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a draft specification or ADR against the spec quality checklist with a fresh context and returns pass or changes required. Use before a spec goes to the human owner. Never edits the document. ([wtf-corp/yaf-backend-framework](https://github.com/wtf-corp/yaf-backend-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews component specs for completeness and consistency with plan ([wurp/utm-stl](https://github.com/wurp/utm-stl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 规格统一审查员（v11，取代逐文档 gate-reviewer）。三份规格文档齐备并通过 spec-lint.sh 后由 /spec-run 或 /spec-tasks 派发，一次派发完成跨文档实质审查，输出 PASS/FAIL。lite.md 不派发本审查（self-check + spec-lint 覆盖）。 ([wynn5a/agent-projects](https://github.com/wynn5a/agent-projects/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review a diff against its originating spec/PRD for fidelity — missing requirements, scope creep, wrong implementation. Dispatch when an originating spec, issue, or PRD exists. Returns severity-ranked findings; reports "no spec available" and exits when there is none. ([Xd06eR/dot-claude](https://github.com/Xd06eR/dot-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Независимый проверяющий. Сверяет спецификацию, код и фактический прогон проверок и пишет отчёт о расхождениях в docs/review/. Ничего не исправляет. Вызывать перед вехой В4, перед мёржем крупных PR и перед защитой. На вход — область проверки (весь проект, список REQ или номер PR). ([yankokirill/Retro](https://github.com/yankokirill/Retro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: SDD 1단계(Requirements) review 게이트. `spec-author`가 작성한 `requirements.md`를 read-only로 검토해 EARS 문법, AC 측정가능성, 중복·누락·충돌, REQ ID 무결성, Glossary 일관성을 검증한다. 위반이 1건이라도 있으면 BLOCK, 모든 항목을 통과하면 PASS로 판정한다. ([yeongdeokcho/crab-harness](https://github.com/yeongdeokcho/crab-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews each spec-kit pipeline artifact for scope drift against the original feature description. Read-only access — cannot modify project files. ([yfr7/dune-tac-toe](https://github.com/yfr7/dune-tac-toe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Specification compliance review agent for checking implementation against design documents and specifications. ([yoshihiko555/Nudge](https://github.com/yoshihiko555/Nudge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: auth/AI/DB/roleplay 등 위험 변경의 스펙 준수 독립 리뷰어. 구현과 다른 시각에서 diff를 스펙(screen/openapi/data-model)과 대조해 누락·위반·edge case 미처리를 찾는다. 읽기 전용 — 코드를 고치지 않는다. ([yyj9529/nextmadi](https://github.com/yyj9529/nextmadi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 仕様3点セット(requirements.md/design.md/tasks.md)を承認PRに出す前にレビューする専任レビュアー。/spec-reviewの工程から起動される。書き込みツールを持たず、指摘の報告に徹する。 ([zakiyama0108/benriyatool](https://github.com/zakiyama0108/benriyatool/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Independent reviewer of a specforge spec. Give it the path to a spec file and the full text of exactly one lens (from lenses/spec/ or .specforge/lenses/spec/), never the author's reasoning or session context. It reads the codebase to judge the spec. Returns findings for the caller to triage. ([zalmb8/specforge](https://github.com/zalmb8/specforge/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a change against its feature spec, criterion by criterion, and reports evidence. Use after a feature is built and before it is called done or merged. ([Zeeshan138063/spec-driven-kit](https://github.com/Zeeshan138063/spec-driven-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks behaviour, on-disk/wire formats, and delivery claims against the specification. Use before closing a change or claiming platform coverage. ([zentelligence/auto-updater](https://github.com/zentelligence/auto-updater/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a change against its page or journey spec in docs/ (structure, states, words, scope). Use after building or changing a page. Read-only. ([zohaf/lineup-bridge](https://github.com/zohaf/lineup-bridge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review specification documents for consistency, completeness, and testability. Use after discovery, before planning. ([Zxela/claude-create](https://github.com/Zxela/claude-create/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer-a**: Requirements and acceptance-coverage reviewer for the specification review gate. Fresh, read-only context; returns structured findings only. ([aharada54914/sdd-forge](https://github.com/aharada54914/sdd-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-review-loop/agents/spec-reviewer-a.md))
- **spec-reviewer-agent**: Use this agent to review a pinned diff against the Spec axis (missing requirements, scope creep, false implementation, and S4 test evidence against applicable acceptance points) of code-review-protocol. Resolves the 4-level spec source and returns High/Medium/Low findings with file:line evidence. Trigger when arthurpower:code-review dispatches the Spec axis. ([winter6205/arthurpower](https://github.com/winter6205/arthurpower/blob/HEAD/arthurpower/agents/spec-reviewer-agent.md) · ★2)
- **spec-reviewer-agent**: You are a meticulous reviewer checking whether an implementation matches its specification. ([badrusiddique/enggenie-skill](https://github.com/badrusiddique/enggenie-skill/blob/HEAD/skills/dev-implement/agents/spec-reviewer-agent.md))
- **spec-reviewer-agent**: You are a senior product engineer reviewing a specification for completeness, clarity, and testability before it goes to engineering. ([badrusiddique/enggenie-skill](https://github.com/badrusiddique/enggenie-skill/blob/HEAD/skills/pm-refine/agents/spec-reviewer-agent.md))
- **spec-reviewer-alt**: Alternate major adversarial specification-conformance review for Gate 1 and Gate 3, read-only. Prefer diversity; use a fresh assigned fallback when unavailable. Cannot accept the change. ([Varadd9406/workflow](https://github.com/Varadd9406/workflow/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/spec-reviewer-alt.md))
- **spec-reviewer-b**: Independent specification risk and ambiguity reviewer for the specification review gate. Fresh, read-only context; receives only counts-and-IDs summary from reviewer A. ([aharada54914/sdd-forge](https://github.com/aharada54914/sdd-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-review-loop/agents/spec-reviewer-b.md))
- **spec-reviewer-conventions**: Review an engineering spec for adherence to repo conventions and prior decisions. Reads CLAUDE.md, the project wiki (if any), and greps the codebase for established patterns. Flags premature abstractions, contradictions with prior decisions, and unneeded backwards-compat shims. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-conventions.md))
- **spec-reviewer-correctness**: Review an engineering spec for correctness — does the proposed implementation actually solve the problem the brief states? Verifies every claim about current code by reading the actual files. Surfaces internal contradictions, references to nonexistent symbols, unsatisfied acceptance criteria, and stale anchors. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-correctness.md))
- **spec-reviewer-edge-cases**: Review an engineering spec for edge cases and failure modes. Probes empty/null/zero-length inputs, concurrency, external-system failures, limits, runtime-precondition violations, and debuggable-tripwire requirements. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-edge-cases.md))
- **spec-reviewer-executor**: PROACTIVELY 仕様書を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性・未定義要素・制約違反を検出して、誰が実装しても同じ結果になる決定論的仕様への書き換えを発行する仕様検証専門エージェント。 以下のいずれかに該当する場合に使用する。 - 仕様書（要件定義書 / 基本設計書 / 内部設計書 / API 仕様書 等）を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性を網羅的に検出したい - 仕様内の未定義要素（暗黙の前提・欠落条件・例外未定義）を抽出したい - 仕様が「誰が書いても同じ実装になる」レベルの決定論性を満たすか判定したい - 制約違反（ネットワーク障害・並行性・分散システム前提）を検出したい - 失敗定義（失敗種別・検出方法・システム挙動）の網羅性を検証したい - 仕様を決定論的状態（曖昧性 0 / 単一実装 / 計測可能値）に書き換えたい - 保証境界（保証対象 / 非保証対象 / 条件）を明示化したい - 仕様の最終判定（決定論的 / テスト可能 / 実装可能）を取得したい 使用しないケース：仕様の作成・抽出そのもの（→ spec-items-clarifier スキル）／コード実装の検証（→ code-review-executor）／設計判断のみで仕様文書が存在しない場合（→ system-basic-design / clean-architecture スキル）／仕様書の単純な構文・誤字修正のみ… ([YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData](https://github.com/YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer-executor.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer-ko**: implementer-ko 산출 코드가 acceptance-criteria.md 와 spec.md 에 정확히 부합하는지 검증하는 specops-ko Phase B Evaluator. 코드 품질이 아닌 "스펙 준수" 만 평가. ([andyko18/devFSD](https://github.com/andyko18/devFSD/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-ko.md))
- **spec-reviewer-lead**: Judges whether one written specification may enter development, at a scale one context cannot hold — splits the admission register's families across spec-reviewer children, holds contradiction and scope itself, checks what comes back and signs one verdict. Judges only; holds no editing tools. Runs before the pipeline, never inside it. ([Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin](https://github.com/Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer-lead.md))

([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-scalability.md))
- **spec-reviewer.agent**: Read-only reviewer for Shortlink blueprint documents. Úsalo para dar veredicto sobre una spec antes de aprobarla, un plan antes de implementarlo o una implementación contra su spec (con el informe del verificador). Hallazgos priorizados con evidencia y arreglo. No edita nada. ([freddysae0/co-creators](https://github.com/freddysae0/co-creators/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd/agents/spec-revisor.md))
- **spec-risk**: Субагент для анализа рисков в спецификациях. НЕ вызывай напрямую — используется через /spec-review команду. Анализирует: технические риски, бизнес-риски, зависимости от внешних факторов, что может пойти не так, mitigation strategies. - **spec-sampler**: 規格覆蓋度取樣 agent。讀一組指定的原始碼檔，逐一識別業務邏輯區塊（SCB）並比對行為規格的覆蓋度與正確性，回傳結構化取樣報告。由 sample-verify skill 分組後平行派發，不主動觸發。唯讀，不改任何檔。 ([KerryHuang/kerryhuang-plugins](https://github.com/KerryHuang/kerryhuang-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sa-flow/agents/spec-sampler.md))

Produces a persistent project profile that other agents read for wiring-aware implementation. Other agents read this profile to generate wiring-aware specs and code. ([Habib0x0/spec-driven-plugin](https://github.com/Habib0x0/spec-driven-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/spec-scanner.md) · ★10)
